答案：D
解析：考查动词，修饰后句"just yet"（恰好现在），那么我不满现状而加倍努力训练，现在又说恰好现在的状态我不对自己太过苛责，那么首先可排除"支持"，无关选项；而因为我在发奋努力训练，现在"想要"或者"支持"的东西自然是要严格要求的，那么排除A、B，最终选择D，不对自己恰在此时知道的东西过多苛责。

B. orders
C. rules
D. games

Very soon the competitive __50__ in me was winning over my lack of confidence.

#### 第50题

A. role
B. part
C. mind
D. value
答案：B

With time, I learned how to play and made friends in the __51__—friends who respected my efforts to work hard and be a team player.

With my __52__ self-confidence comes more praise from teachers and classmates.

#### 第52题

### 2011年江苏卷

A boy was walking home from school when he saw a large, tempting（诱人的）apple on one of the branches of an apple tree hanging out over a tall fence. The boy wasn't much of a fruit-eater, __36__ a bar of chocolate if given the choice, __37__, as they say, the forbidden fruit can be tempting. Seeing the apple, the boy wanted it. The more he looked at it, the __38__ he felt and the more he wanted that apple.
__39__ as high as he could, but even as his tallest __40__ he was unable to touch it. He began to __41__ up and down, as high as he could, at the __42__ of each jump stretching his arms to get the apple. Still it remained out of __43__.

His school bag wouldn't give enough height and he didn't want to __45__ the things inside, like his lunch box, pencil case, and Gameboy. Looking __46__, he hoped he might find an old box, a rock, or, __47__ luck, even a ladder, but it was a tidy neighborhood and there was nothing he could use.
He had tried everything he could think to do. __48__ seeing any other choices, he gave up and started to walk __49__. At first he felt angry and disappointed thinking about how hungry he had become from his __50__, and how he really wanted that apple. The more he __51__ like this, the more unhappy he became.
__52__, the boy of our story was a pretty smart guy, even if he couldn't always get what he wanted. He started to say to himself, "This isn't __53__. I don't have the apple and I'm feeling miserable as well. There's __54__ more I can do to get the apple—that is unchangeable—but we are supposed to be able to __55__ our feelings. ### 2011年山东卷

### 2011年四川卷

阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的四个选项(A, B, C和D）中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。
I truly feel that my mother led me here, to Morzaine, and to my future as happy wife and businesswoman. When Mum __21__ in October 2007, I was a cook. In December that year, while I was working for a wedding, a pearl necklace Mum had left me __22__. I was distraught（忧心如焚的）. Some days later, I was __23__ that a guy who was working with us that day "could probably have made a fortune __24__ the necklace he found." __25__, he returned it. Hearing I'd __26__ Mum for six months before her death, he said, "Christmas is going to be __27__—why not go out to the Alps for a couple of weeks?"
I came to Morzaine, a small, friendly village in the Alps and __28__ fell in love with it. What was __29__ to be a stopgap (权宜之计) trip turned into a new life. I kept travelling between London and here and felt __30__ than I had in months. In December 2008, I was __31__ as a hotel manager and moved here full time.
A month later, I met Paul, who was traveling here. We fell in love. In the beginning, I didn't want to discuss __32__, because the sadness of losing Mum __33__ felt great. Paul understood that and never __34__ me. But, by summer, we got married. A year later, we used his saving, and the money from the sale of Mom's house, to build our own __35__.
We want to give our guests a __36__ feel, so each room is themed (以……为题) around memories from our lives. There are also styles to remind me of Mom—a tiny chair which __37__ be in her bedroom is set in one room.
We are having a wonderful life Mum __38__ naturally part of it, __39__ there's no way we would be here if it wasn't for the __40__ she gave me. I know she's here in spirit, keeping an eye on us.

#### 第21题

A. died
B. came
C. returned
D. visited
答案：A
解析：考查动词词义和语境理解。die去世；come来；return返回，归还；visit拜访。有下文的death可知母亲"去世了"。
In December that year, while I was working for a wedding, a pearl necklace Mum had left me __22__.

#### 第22题

A. burned
B. disappeared
C. broke
D. dropped
答案：B

Some days later, I was __23__ that a guy who was working with us that day...

A. shown
B. comforted
C. persuaded
D. told
答案：D
解析：考查动词词义和语境理解。找了几天没有找到，几天之后别人"告诉"（tell）了我项链的下落。show展示；comfort安慰；persuade劝服。

... "could probably have made a fortune __24__ the necklace he found."

A. hiding
B. stealing
C. selling
D. wearing
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析和语境理解。既然是赚了一大笔钱，捡到项链的那个人肯定是把它卖了。hide隐藏；steal偷（没有证据表明项链是那个人偷走的）；wear穿戴。

__25__, he returned it.

#### 第25题

A. Luckily
B. Naturally
C. Surely
D. Hopefully
答案：A
解析：考查副词词义及语境理解。项链失而复得，"我"是幸运地。luckily幸运地；naturally自然地；surely确定地；hopefully有希望地。

Hearing I'd __26__ Mum for six months before her death, he said...

Christmas is going to be __27__—why not go out to the Alps for a couple of weeks?

I came to Morzaine, a small, friendly village in the Alps and __28__ fell in love with it.

because the sadness of losing Mum __33__ felt great.

### 2011年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）

阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的四个选项（A、B、C和D）中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。（本文主要讲述了一位女士对大约20年前第一次接触自然科学课程的经历。教授让学生猜罐里有多少豆子，并告诉学生不要相信自己的感官，这使当年只有17岁的她很害怕，当天下午就放弃了这门课程，至今也没再走近科学。）
In our discussion with people on how education can help them succeed in life, a woman remembered the first meeting of an introductory __36__ course about 20 years ago.
The professor __37__ the lecture hall, placed upon his desk a large jar filled with dried beans（豆）, and invited the students to __38__ how many beans the jar contained. After __39__ shouts of wildly wrong guesses the professor smiled a thin, dry smile, announced the __40__ answer, and went on saying, "You have just __41__ an important lesson about science. That is: Never __42__ your own senses."
Twenty years later, the __43__ could guess what the professor had in mind. He __44__ himself, perhaps, as inviting his students to start an exciting __45__ into an unknown world invisible（无形的）to the __46__, which can be discovered only through scientific __47__. But the seventeen-year-old girl could not accept or even __48__ the invitation. She was just __49__ to understand the world. And she __50__ that her firsthand experience could be the __51__. The professor, however, said that it was __52__. He was taking away her only __53__ for knowing and was providing her with no substitute. "I remember feeling small and __54__," the woman says, "and I did the only thing I could do. I __55__ the course that afternoon, and I haven't gone near science since."

#### 第36题

A. art
B. history
C. science
D. math
答案：C
解析：考查名词辨析。根据本段上文出现了名词education，以及根据下文可知，这位女士记起的是一堂自然科学课程。
The professor __37__ the lecture hall, placed upon his desk a large jar filled with dried beans.

#### 第37题

A. searched for
B. looked at
C. got through
D. marched into
答案：D
解析：考查动词词组辨析。根据语境可知，此处指教授走进演讲大厅。search for "寻找"；look at "看"；get through "完成，打通电话"；march into "（大步）走进"。

and invited the students to __38__ how many beans the jar contained.

#### 第38题

A. count
B. guess
C. report
D. watch
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。根据下一句中的After shouts of wildly wrong guesses可知，此处指教授让学生猜有多少豆子。

After __39__ shouts of wildly wrong guesses the professor smiled a thin, dry smile.

#### 第39题

A. warning
B. giving
C. turning away
D. listening to
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。根据句子结构可以看出，第39个空中的动作应是教授发出的。句意：听了学生的瞎猜之后，教授露出一个干瘪的笑。

announced the __40__ answer, and went on saying.

#### 第40题

A. ready
B. possible
C. correct
D. difficult
答案：C
解析：考查形容词辨析。根据前面的wildly wrong guesses可知，此处指教授给出正确的答案。

You have just __41__ an important lesson about science.

#### 第41题

A. learned
B. prepared
C. taught
D. taken
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。learn an important lesson表示"学到很重要的一堂课"。根据该动作是学生对课程做出的，那么自然是在学习课程。

That is: Never __42__ your own senses.

#### 第42题

A. lose
B. trust
C. sharpen
D. show
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。根据这个故事可知，教授让学生不要相信自己的感官。

Twenty years later, the __43__ could guess what the professor had in mind.

#### 第43题

A. lecturer
B. scientist
C. speaker
D. woman
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。此处的the woman就是指第一段中的a woman。根据下文也可推断出，因为她提到20年前自己是一个17岁的女生。

He __44__ himself, perhaps, as inviting his students to start an exciting __45__ into an unknown world.

#### 第44题

A. described
B. respected
C. saw
D. served
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析。see…as…"把……看作"；describe…as…"把……描述为"；respect…as…"把……尊为"；serve as"充当"。他可能把自己看作是在邀请学生去探索未知世界。

inviting his students to start an exciting __45__ into an unknown world.

#### 第45题

A. voyage
B. movement
C. change
D. rush
答案：A
解析：考查名词辨析。学生对未知世界开启的是一次航程。voyage航行。

an unknown world invisible（无形的）to the __46__.

#### 第46题

A. professor
B. eye
C. knowledge
D. light
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。根据"an unknown world invisible to the eye"可知，指眼睛看不到的未知的世界。

which can be discovered only through scientific __47__.

#### 第47题

A. model
B. senses
C. spirit
D. methods
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。此处指能通过科学的方法去发现眼睛看不到的未知的世界（而不是通过感官）。

But the seventeen-year-old girl could not accept or even __48__ the invitation.

#### 第48题

A. hear
B. make
C. present
D. refuse
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。even是一个强烈的递进关系词。根据句意可知，但是这个17岁的女生不愿意接受邀请或者甚至听到这样的邀请。

She was just __49__ to understand the world.

#### 第49题

A. suggesting
B. beginning
C. pretending
D. waiting
答案：B

And she __50__ that her firsthand experience could be the __51__.

#### 第50题

A. believed
B. doubted
C. proved
D. explained
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。（因为她自己正开始了解世界）所以她相信直接得到的经验就是真实的。

And she believed that her firsthand experience could be the __51__.

#### 第51题

A. growth
B. strength
C. faith
D. truth
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。女孩认为第一感觉是正确的。growth成长；strength力量；faith信念。此处表示"事实真相"。

The professor, however, said that it was __52__.

#### 第52题

A. firm
B. interesting
C. wrong
D. acceptable
答案：C
解析：考查形容词辨析。根据上一段可知，教授认为不要相信感官，因为感官带来的答案是错误的。

He was taking away her only __53__ for knowing and was providing her with no substitute.

#### 第53题

A. task
B. tool
C. success
D. connection
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。她认为教授把她依赖的唯一的工具即感官拿走了。

"I remember feeling small and __54__," the woman says.

#### 第54题

A. cruel
B. proud
C. frightened
D. brave
答案：C
解析：考查形容词辨析。and提示文章此处是并列关系，而与感觉渺小并列的是感觉害怕。根据女士的描述可知，她当时认为自己很渺小，很害怕。

I __55__ the course that afternoon, and I haven't gone near science since.

#### 第55题

A. dropped
B. started
C. passed
D. missed
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。drop在此表示"丢弃，中断，停止"。被教授伤害后，我当天下午就放弃了这门课程。

## 2012年

### 2012年北京卷

阅读下面短文，掌握其大意，从每题所给的A、B、C、D四个选项中，选出最佳选项。
Inspiration
"Mama, when I grow up, I'm going to be one of those!" I said this after seeing the Capital Dancing Company perform when I was three. It was the first time that my __36__ took on a vivid form and acted as something important to start my training. As I grew older and was __37__ to more, my interests in the world of dance __38__ varied but that little girl's dream of someday becoming a __39__ in the company never left me. In the summer of 2005 when I was 18, I received the phone call which made that dream a __40__; I became a member of the company __41__ back to 1925.
As I look back on that day now, it surely __42__ any sense of reality. I believe I stayed in a state of pleasant disbelief __43__ I was halfway through rehearsals (排练) on my first day. I never actually __44__ to get the job. After being offered the position, I was completely __45__. I remember shaking with excitement.
Though I was absolutely thrilled with the change, it did not come without its fair share of __46__. Through the strict rehearsal period of dancing six days a week, I found it vital to __47__ up the material fast with every last bit of concentration. It is that extreme __48__ to detail (细节) and stress on practice that set us __49__. To then follow those high-energy rehearsals __50__ a busy show schedule of up to five performances a day, I discovered a new __51__ of the words "hard work." What I thought were my physical __52__ were pushed much further than I thought __53__. I learned to make each performance better than the last.
Today, when I look at the unbelievable company that I have the great __54__ of being a part of, not only as a member, but as a dance captain, I see a __55__ that has inspired not only generations of little girls but a splendid company that continues to develop and grow — and inspires people every day to follow their dreams.

#### 第36题

A. hobby
B. plan
C. dream
D. word
答案：C
解析：三岁看舞蹈表演时，梦想初步形成，与文章后面提到梦想实现呼应。hobby业余爱好；plan计划；word单词，均与文章不符。
As I grew older and was __37__ to more, my interests in the world of dance __38__ varied but that little girl's dream of someday becoming a __39__ in the company never left me.

#### 第37题

A. connected
B. expanded
C. exposed
D. extended
答案：C
解析：be exposed to接触。这里的意思是在长大的过程中，"我"接触到更多的东西，对舞蹈的兴趣也有了改变，但成为该公司舞蹈家的梦想没有改变。connect连接；expand扩展；extend延伸。

As I grew older and was __37__ to more, my interests in the world of dance __38__ varied but that little girl's dream of someday becoming a __39__ in the company never left me.

#### 第38题

A. rarely
B. certainly
C. probably
D. consistently
答案：B
解析：certainly确实，当然；rarely很少地；probably可能；consistently一贯地。本句中作者的兴趣确实会随着年龄和阅历的增加而改变。

...but that little girl's dream of someday becoming a __39__ in the company never left me.

#### 第39题

A. director
B. trainer
C. leader
D. dancer
答案：D
解析：作者从三岁就有了想成为舞蹈家的梦想，这里还是原意的延伸。director导演；trainer教练员；leader领导。

In the summer of 2005 when I was 18, I received the phone call which made that dream a __40__;

#### 第40题

A. symbol
B. memory
C. truth
D. reality
答案：D
解析：这里作者接到了舞蹈公司的电话，从而使自己的梦想变成了现实。make that dream a reality使梦想变成现实。symbol符号；memory回忆；truth真理。

I became a member of the company __41__ back to 1925.

#### 第41题

A. bouncing
B. dating
C. turning
D. tracking
答案：B
解析：date back to=date from起源于，追溯到。这里用现在分词短语作定语，说明公司成立于1925年。bounce弹跳；turn转身；track追踪。

As I look back on that day now, it surely __42__ any sense of reality.

#### 第42题

A. lacks
B. adds
C. makes
D. brings
答案：A
解析：上一段作者谈到被招为舞蹈团成员，梦想即将成真。对照下文，作者其实一直处于一种不相信的喜悦中。lack缺乏，其他选项均不符合语境。

I believe I stayed in a state of pleasant disbelief __43__ I was halfway through rehearsals on my first day.

#### 第43题

A. while
B. since
C. until
D. when
答案：C
解析：这里的意思是说自己的这种不相信的喜悦一直到当天排练的中间才消失。until直到。

I never actually __44__ to get the job.

#### 第44题

A. cared
B. expected
C. asked
D. decided
答案：B
解析：本句进一步解释接到通知当天不相信的喜悦，是因为自己没有预料到会得到这份工作。care在乎；expect期望，预料；ask要求；decide决定。

After being offered the position, I was completely __45__.

#### 第45题

A. motivated
B. relaxed
C. convinced
D. astonished
答案：D
解析：本句继续说明自己当时的精神状态是惊讶。motivated有积极性的；relaxed放松的；convinced确信的；astonished吃惊的。

Though I was absolutely thrilled with the change, it did not come without its fair share of __46__.

#### 第46题

A. challenges
B. profits
C. advantages
D. adventures
答案：A
解析：本段开始用了一个转折句，说明尽管"我"带着不相信的喜悦参加第一天的训练，但困难和挑战一点也没减少。challenge挑战；profit利润；advantage优势；adventure冒险。

I found it vital to __47__ up the material fast with every last bit of concentration.

#### 第47题

A. put
B. mix
C. build
D. pick
答案：D
解析：pick up这里的意思是"学会，掌握"。句意：我发现集中精力尽快学好这些材料至关重要。put up举起；mix up混合；build up加强。

It is that extreme __48__ to detail (细节) and stress on practice that set us __49__.

#### 第48题

A. attention
B. association
C. attraction
D. adaptation
答案：A
解析：承接上句集中精力，这里是想说对细节的关注和训练的压力导致参与舞蹈的人的分化，即我脱颖而出的原因。attention关注；association联合；attraction吸引；adaptation适应。

It is that extreme __48__ to detail (细节) and stress on practice that set us __49__.

#### 第49题

A. apart
B. aside
C. off
D. back
答案：A
解析：set sb apart使分离，区分开；set aside省出，撤销，不顾；set off出发；set back使推迟。

To then follow those high-energy rehearsals __50__ a busy show schedule of up to five performances a day, I discovered a new __51__ of the words "hard work."

#### 第50题

A. over
B. by
C. with
D. beyond
答案：C
解析：句意：每天都有高能量的训练和每天达到五场演出的时间安排，让我体会了"hard work"的新含义。with表示随着。

To then follow those high-energy rehearsals __50__ a busy show schedule of up to five performances a day, I discovered a new __51__ of the words "hard work."

#### 第51题

A. function
B. meaning
C. expression
D. usage
答案：B
解析：根据空格前后的句子可知，作者终于理解了"hard work"的新含义，自己的潜能被发挥到极致。function功能；meaning意思；expression表达；usage用法。

What I thought were my physical __52__ were pushed much further than I thought __53__.

#### 第52题

A. boundaries
B. problems
C. barriers
D. efforts
答案：A
解析：这里的句子是说作者每天都有高能量的训练和演出，让作者知道了她体能的潜力被发挥到超出想象的地步。boundary边界，极限；problem问题；barrier障碍；effort努力。

What I thought were my physical __52__ were pushed much further than I thought __53__.

#### 第53题

A. necessary
B. perfect
C. proper
D. possible
答案：D
解析：necessary必要的；perfect完美的；proper恰当的；possible可能的。这里指超过了所认为可能的地步。

Today, when I look at the unbelievable company that I have the great __54__ of being a part of, not only as a member, but as a dance captain,

#### 第54题

A. talent
B. honor
C. potential
D. responsibility
答案：B
解析：在自己梦寐以求的舞蹈团中，不仅作为一个成员而且还是队长，对于作者来说是一种很高的荣誉。talent才能；honor荣誉；potential潜能；responsibility责任。

I see a __55__ that has inspired not only generations of little girls but a splendid company that continues to develop and grow — and inspires people every day to follow their dreams.

D. desire

In this situation, the victim was more likely to __52__ aid if his face was spotless than if he had an unattractive birthmark. The application was ready to be __57__, but had apparently been "lost". The photo attached to the application was sometimes that of a very __58__ person and sometimes that of a less attractive person. #### 第62题

Lightning flashed through the darkness over Sibson's bedroom skylight（天窗）. Sibson was shaken by a clap of thunder __21__ he knew what was happening. The storm had moved directly __22__ his two-story wooden house. Then he heard the smoke alarm beeping.
Sibson rushed down the stairs barefoot to __23__; he opened the door to the basement（地下室）, and flames __24__ out. Sibson ran back upstairs to call 911 from his bedroom. "I felt __25__ because the room had a separate outdoor stairway," he explains.
But the phone didn't work, and when he tried to go down the outdoor stairway, he was __26__ by a wall of flames. Sibson realized he was trapped（困住）.
Sibson's house was three kilometers __27__ the main road and was so well hidden by trees that he knew calling for help would be __28__.
Up a hill nearby lived Sibson's neighbor, Huggons. He was lying in bed when something like a smoke alarm __29__ his ears. He jumped out of bed, took his __30__ and flashlight, and headed down the hillside toward the __31__. That was when he saw the rolling heavy smoke.
Huggons dialed 911, and the operator warned him not to __32__ the house. But Huggons said, "There is no way I am going to listen to Sibson __33__ and die in that fire."
"Anyone there?" Huggons called out. Then he heard "Help! I'm trapped!" coming from the second floor balcony（阳台）. He entered the house, but soon had to run back to catch his __34__.
After one more __35__ inside the house, Huggons gave up and __36__ around back.
The wind parted the smoke just __37__ for him to catch sight of Sibson. But there was no way to get to him. He __38__ the flashlight into the woods and noticed a ladder. He took it over to the balcony and __39__ Sibson down just as the second floor of the house fell off.

B. while
C. since

The storm had moved directly __22__ his two-story wooden house.

## 2013年

### 2013年北京卷

阅读下面短文，掌握其大意，从每题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出最佳选项。
A Leap（跳跃）to Honor
Leaping on a narrow balance beam（平衡木）is not easy. But Lola Walter, a 13-year-old gymnast, is an expert at it.
To perfect her skills, Lola __36__ for four hours a day, five days a week. At the state championships in March, she finished seventh out of 16 girls. That's especially impressive, __37__ she is legally blind, born with a rare condition that causes her eyes to shift constantly. She often sees double and can't __38__ how far away things are.
When she was little, her mom __39__ that even though she couldn't see __40__, she was fearless. So her mom signed her up for gymnastics when she was three. She loved the __41__ right away and gymnastics became her favorite. Though learning gymnastics has been more __42__ for her than for some of her teammates, she has never quit. She doesn't let her __43__ stop her from doing anything that she wants to.
She likes the determination it takes to do the sport. Her biggest __44__ is the balance beam. Because she has double vision, she often sees two beams. She must use her sense of touch to help her during her routine. Sometimes she even closes her eyes. "You have to __45__ your mind that it'll take you where you want to go." says Lola.
To be a top-level gymnast, one must be brave. The beam is probably the most __46__ for anyone because it's four inches wide. At the state competition, Lola didn't fall __47__ the beam. In fact, she got an 8.1 out of 10—her highest score yet.
Lola doesn't want to be __48__ differently from the other girls on her team. At the competitions, the judges don't know about her vision __49__. She doesn't tell them, because she doesn't think they need to know. Her mom is amazed by her __50__ attitude.
Lola never thinks about __51__. She is presently at level 7 while the highest is level 10 in gymnastics. Her __52__ is to reach level 9. She says she wants to be a gymnastics coach to pass down what she's learned to other kids __53__ she grew up.
Lola is __54__ of all her hard work and success. She says it's helped her overcome problems in her life outside gymnastics, too. Her __55__ for others is "just believe yourself".

#### 第36题

A. runs
B. teaches
C. trains
D. dances
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析。根据上一句中的 a 13-year-old gymnast可知，To perfect her skills为了练习她的体操技巧，她每天训练（train）四个小时。
#### 第37题

A. since
B. unless
C. after
D. though
答案：A
解析：考查连词辨析。根据she is legally blind可知，因为她在法定上讲是盲人，这是她取得的成绩给人留下深刻印象的原因。
#### 第38题

A. tell
B. guess
C. assume
D. predict
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。根据前一句She often sees double可知，她看到的是双重影像，因此无法辨别出物体离她有多远。
#### 第39题

A. suspected
B. remembered
C. imagined
D. noticed
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。在她小的时候，她的妈妈注意到她尽管看不清东西，但是她不害怕。
#### 第40题

A. deeply
B. well
C. ahead
D. closely
答案：B
解析：考查副词辨析。根据上文的"born with a rare condition that causes her eyes to shift constantly. She often sees double"可知，她是能看到东西的，只不过看到的是双重影像，看不清楚。
#### 第41题

A. task
B. sport
C. event
D. show
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。根据上文的"So her mom signed her up for gymnastics when she was three"可知，她立刻喜欢上了这项运动。
#### 第42题

A. boring
B. enjoyable
C. different
D. unsatisfactory
答案：C
解析：考查形容词辨析。根据该句的though（尽管），和后文的she has never quit可知，尽管学习体操对她来说比其他孩子更加困难，但是她从没有放弃过。
#### 第43题

A. talent
B. quality
C. nature
D. condition
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。根据上文的"she is legally blind, born with a rare condition that causes her eyes to shift constantly"可知，此处指的是她视力差的这种不利状况（情况）。
#### 第44题

A. doubt
B. advantage
C. challenge
D. program
答案：C
解析：考查名词辨析。根据下文的"Because she has double vision, she often sees two beams"可知，平衡木对她来说是最大的一个挑战。
#### 第45题

A. examine
B. express
C. open
D. trust
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。她视力不好，只能依靠自己的感觉，因此她说"你不得不相信（trust）你的内心，它可以带你去任何你想去的地方"。
#### 第46题

A. fearful
B. harmful
C. unfair
D. inconvenient
答案：A
解析：考查形容词辨析。要想成为一个顶尖的体操运动员必须勇敢，需要勇气，就说明平衡木这项运动让人害怕。
#### 第47题

A. to
B. on
C. off
D. against
答案：C
解析：考查介词辨析。在州比赛中，Lola没有从平衡木上跌落下来。fall off 从……跌落。
#### 第48题

A. greeted
B. treated
C. served
D. paid
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。她在比赛的时候没有告诉裁判关于她视力方面的问题，因为她不想让裁判做出有别于其他选手的对待。
#### 第49题

A. pains
B. stresses
C. injuries
D. problems
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。她在比赛的时候没有告诉裁判关于她视力方面的问题，vision problems意为视力上的问题。
#### 第50题

A. positive
B. friendly
C. flexible
D. cautious
答案：A
解析：考查形容词辨析。她没有因为眼睛视力的问题而沮丧，相反她保持积极的态度。她的母亲对她的这种积极、乐观的态度感到很惊讶。
#### 第51题

A. defending
B. quitting
C. winning
D. bargaining
答案：B
解析：考查动名词辨析。她的目标是要达到九级，她说她要成为一名体操教练，因此她从没想过要放弃（quit）。
#### 第52题

A. standard
B. range
C. view
D. goal
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。现在她达到了七级水平，而此处to reach level 9则指的是她的目标，即goal。
#### 第53题

A. until
B. as
C. when
D. before
答案：C
解析：考查连词辨析。她说她想要成为一名体操教练，把她所学到的传给其他的孩子，当她长大成人的时候（when）。
#### 第54题

A. proud
B. tired
C. ashamed
D. confident
答案：A
解析：考查形容词辨析。Lola以她的努力和成功为傲。be proud of 以……为傲。
#### 第55题

A. plan
B. advice
C. reward
D. responsibility
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。她通过自己的亲身经历，给人们一个善意的建议，就是"相信自己"。
阅读下面短文，掌握其大意，然后从16～35各题所给出的A、B、C、D四个选项中选出最佳选项。
As I held my father's hands one night, I couldn't help but notice their calluses (老茧) and roughness. His hands tell the story of his life as a __16__, including all his struggles.
One summer, I remember, a drought (旱灾) hit Ontario, turning it into a __17__ desert. On one of those hot mornings I was picking sweet corn with my dad to fill the last __18__ from the grocery store. Fifty dozen was all we needed, which __19__ took twenty minutes. That morning, however, the process didn't __20__ quickly. After forty minutes of aimlessly walking in the field, we __21__ needed twenty dozen. I was completely frustrated and __22__. Dropping the basket heavily, I declared, "If the store wants its last twenty dozen, they can pick it themselves!" Dad __23__. "Just think, my little girl, only ten dozen left for each of us and then we're __24__." Such is Dad—whatever problem he __25__, he never gives up.
__26__, the disastrous effects of the drought were felt all over our county. It was a challenging time for everyone, __27__ Dad remained optimistic. He __28__ to be grateful for other things like good health and food on our plates. Only then did I truly begin to __29__ Dad and his faith that guided us through the hard times.
Dad is also a living example of real __30__. From dawn to dusk, he walks countless hours to __31__ our family. He always puts our happiness __32__ his own, and never fails to cheer me on at my sports games __33__ his exhaustion after long days. His loving and selfless nature has inspired me to become more sympathetic and __34__, putting others first.
Dad, the life __35__ I have learned from you will stay with me forever. You are my father, teacher, friend and, most importantly, my hero.
#### 第16题

A. teacher
B. gardener
C. farmer
D. grocer
答案：C
解析：根据下文提到了"picking sweet corn with my dad"、"in the field"并结合上文对父亲的手的描写"their calluses and roughness"可知，作者的父亲应是一位农民。
#### 第17题

A. stormy
B. lively
C. disappearing
D. burning
答案：D
解析：根据上一句"a drought hit Ontario"可知，安大略省发生了旱灾，这里指的应是该地区变成了燃烧的沙漠。
#### 第18题

A. order
B. form
C. gap
D. position
答案：A
解析：结合上下文可知，作者与父亲在地里掰甜玉米是为了交付杂货店的最后一笔订单；fill an order为固定短语，意为交付订货。
#### 第19题

A. repeatedly
B. normally
C. finally
D. really
答案：B
解析：根据下一句"然而，那天早上，这一过程进展地并不快"可知，此处表达的应是在通常情况下，即50打通常只需要20分钟。
#### 第20题

A. go
B. begin
C. occur
D. change
答案：A
解析：根据上一句"50打通常只需要20分钟"可推测出，本句的意思是：然而，那天早上，这一过程进展地并不快。go有"进展"的意思。
#### 第21题

A. yet
B. still
C. even
D. nearly
答案：B
解析：根据上文提到"Fifty dozen was all we needed"以及下文"I was completely frustrated"可知，这里应表示"我们仍然还需要20打"。
#### 第22题

A. surprised
B. nervous
C. angry
D. frightened
答案：C
解析：根据下文"Dropping the basket heavily, I declared..."可知，此处表明了我的愤怒。
#### 第23题

A. apologized
B. cried
C. complained
D. laughed
答案：D
解析：根据下文父亲开导我的话以及"he never gives up"可知，这时父亲应是和颜悦色或是笑着对我说。
#### 第24题

A. lost
B. done
C. gone
D. touched
答案：B
解析：结合上下文可知，本句的意思应是：我们每人只剩下10打，然后我们就做完了。
#### 第25题

A. meets with
B. brings up
C. works out
D. thinks about
答案：A
解析：本句的意思是：无论他碰到什么样的难题，他都从不放弃。meet with 碰到、遭受。
#### 第26题

A. Thankfully
B. Hopefully
C. Unfortunately
D. Strangely
答案：C
解析：根据下一句"the disastrous effects of the drought were felt all over our county"表达的是很糟的情况，即干旱所带来的灾难性的影响遍布了全省。
#### 第27题

A. or
B. for
C. so
D. but
答案：D
解析：分析前后两句的句意可知，此处表达的应是转折关系：虽然对每个人来说，这都是一个具有挑战性的时刻，但是爸爸仍然很乐观。
#### 第28题

A. happened
B. seemed
C. continued
D. aimed
答案：C
解析：本句描述的是父亲仍很乐观的具体表现：他继续对其他的事物充满了感激，如良好的健康状况以及我们盘中的食物。
#### 第29题

A. face
B. appreciate
C. examine
D. question
答案：B
解析：此处应填动词appreciate，表达了作者对父亲以及引导他们度过困难时期的信念的感激。
#### 第30题

A. love
B. pride
C. friendship
D. honesty
答案：A
解析：通读本段可知，本句是该段的中心句；结合下文"His loving and selfless nature"可知，本段主要赞扬了父亲对家人无私的爱。
#### 第31题

A. support
B. settle
C. start
D. impress
答案：A
解析：结合上文可知，此处应表示父亲从早到晚，工作无数个小时来支撑着我们的家庭。support 支撑、支持。
#### 第32题

A. after
B. before
C. beside
D. under
答案：B
解析：根据本段主要讲述了父亲无私的爱可知，本句的意思应是：他总是将我们的幸福放在他自己的前面。
#### 第33题

A. in spite of
B. in terms of
C. in control of
D. in place of
答案：A
解析：根据前后两句的句意关系可知，此处应表示转折：尽管他经历了长时间的疲劳，他从不会忘记在我参加运动会时为我加油。in spite of 虽然、尽管。
#### 第34题

A. careful
B. regretful
C. considerate
D. humorous
答案：C
解析：根据句中的"putting others first"可推测出，此处应学会体谅他人，使自己变得更具同情心，并且更体贴。considerate 体贴的。
#### 第35题

A. history
B. motto
C. patterns
D. lessons
答案：D
解析：根据文中提到"Dad is also a living example of..."父亲是她学习的榜样以及"You are my father, teacher..."父亲是我的老师可知，此处指的应是父亲给她上的人生功课。

### 2013年江苏卷

#### 第36题

A. curious about
B. interested in
C. aware of
D. careful with
答案：C
解析：考查形容词短语辨析。根据空白处后面的the problems faced by disabled people可知，活动的目的是为了"意识"到残疾人面临的问题。
#### 第37题

A. cure
B. prevent
C. adopt
D. analyze
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析。根据下文some members chose…others wore…可知，作者被要求"挑选/采用"一种残疾，而不是治愈、阻止或分析。
#### 第38题

A. instead
B. strangely
C. as usual
D. like me
答案：D
解析：考查上下文语境理解。根据最后一段第一句中的my wheelchair experiment可知，作者选择使用轮椅，即"像我一样"的成员选了轮椅。
#### 第39题

A. learning
B. working
C. satisfying
D. relaxing
答案：A
解析：考查形容词辨析。根据后句I had never considered before how…和这段最后一句的感慨可知，作者认为坐在轮椅上就是一次"学习"的经历。
#### 第40题

A. convenient
B. awkward
C. boring
D. exciting
答案：B
解析：考查形容词辨析。根据第二段的全段描述，活动前作者从未意识到使用轮椅有多么的"笨拙"。
#### 第41题

A. height
B. force
C. skill
D. weight
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。as soon as I sat down提示，轮子动起来发生的时间是当作者一坐下去的时候，显然能让轮子动起来的是作者的体重。
#### 第42题

A. locked
B. repaired
C. powered
D. grasped
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。后句then提示：本句是对上句轮子为什么会转动的解释，显然因为轮子未被锁住。
#### 第43题

A. hands
B. feet
C. keys
D. handles
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。根据下文"费了好长时间我才让金属footrest（脚蹬）归位"可知，这时的作者不知道把自己的脚放在哪里。
#### 第44题

A. place
B. action
C. play
D. effect
答案：A
解析：考查固定短语。put…into place意为"把……归位"。
#### 第45题

A. operation
B. communication
C. transportation
D. production
答案：C
解析：考查名词辨析。作者参加的是使用轮椅几小时的体验活动，而轮椅是一种交通工具。means of transportation 交通工具。
#### 第46题

A. exploration
B. education
C. experiment
D. entertainment
答案：C
解析：考查名词辨析。根据第一段可知，作者参加的是残疾体验活动，使用轮椅只不过是个实验，但对残疾人而言，使用轮椅不再是个实验，而是一辈子的生活。
#### 第47题

A. flexible
B. safe
C. starting
D. comfortable
答案：D
解析：考查形容词辨析。根据and后面的thought it might be restful可知，作者努力要找一个舒服的姿势。
#### 第48题

A. yet
B. just
C. still
D. even
答案：D
解析：考查副词辨析。相比空白处前面的restful，空白处后面的kind of nice显然递进了一步，所以最佳答案是even，"甚至"。
#### 第49题

A. shown
B. pushed
C. driven
D. guided
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。作者坐着轮椅，自然希望被人推，而不是开车接送或引导。
#### 第50题

A. realized
B. suggested
C. agreed
D. admitted
答案：A

B. turned
C. pressed

B. heavy
C. major

A. forgotten
B. repeated
C. conducted
D. finished
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。作者要感慨残疾人的不易，自然要到体验结束。
#### 第55题

B. challenges
C. anxieties
D. illnesses
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。"我"只是"品尝"了一下残疾人必须要克服的心理和身体上的挑战。

解析：从前面的even though 引导的让步状语从句可以看出作者仍然觉得听到那些外号觉得糟糕。
#### 第13题

A. hunger
B. cold
C. defeats
D. burns
答案：D
解析：整整一碗滚烫的油掉在头上应该是烧伤（burns）。
#### 第14题

A. rushed
B. led
C. invited
D. forced
答案：A
解析：be rushed to 被紧急送到；be led to 被带到；be invited to 被邀请到；be forced to 被迫到。作者烧伤后，应该是被紧急送到医院。
#### 第15题

A. learned
B. fought
C. returned
D. decided
答案：B
解析：根据文意，应该是医生努力地拯救了我的生命，而不是才学着做或者入院几周了才决定拯救作者的生命。fight to do 努力做、拼命做。
#### 第16题

A. happy
B. lucky
C. lonely
D. poor
答案：B
解析：医生肯定是告诉作者父母作者能活下来很幸运（lucky）。
#### 第17题

A. pressed
B. occupied
C. left
D. painted
答案：C
解析：be left with 被留下。本题应该是头上留下了伤痕。
#### 第18题

A. possibly
B. usually
C. finally
D. nearly
答案：B
解析：我经常戴着头巾，usually 经常性地。
#### 第19题

A. Although
B. Since
C. If
D. Before
答案：C
解析：后半句说人们就会叫我一些难听的名字比如"秃子"，所以前面应该是条件状语从句，用if 引导。
#### 第20题

A. correctly
B. roughly
C. easily
D. really
答案：D
解析：即使我的朋友经常安慰我，他们从未真正（really）理解那是什么感觉。correctly 正确地；roughly 大概地；easily 简单地。
#### 第21题

A. promoted
B. introduced
C. reported
D. carried
答案：B
解析：be introduced to 被引荐到，被介绍到。作者是经别人介绍去的儿童烧伤夏令营。
#### 第22题

A. met
B. recognized
C. remembered
D. caught
答案：A
解析：在那里我遇到（met）了14岁的Stephanie。
#### 第23题

A. honest
B. strong
C. active
D. young
答案：B
解析：Stephanie 从不被任何人打垮，可见她是很坚强的（strong）。
#### 第24题

A. write down
B. agree with
C. pass on
D. listen to
答案：D
解析：后半句说"我们和别人没什么不同"，所以是不要听（listen to）别人怎么说。
#### 第25题

A. promised
B. encouraged
C. ordered
D. calmed
答案：B
解析：根据题意，Stephanie 应该是鼓励（encouraged）我。
#### 第26题

A. in
B. for
C. without
D. beyond
答案：C
解析：Stephanie告诉作者，即使不戴头巾也很漂亮，句末的it 指的是头巾scarf，所以不戴头巾是without the scarf。
#### 第27题

A. similar
B. strange
C. hard
D. important
答案：A
解析：Stephanie和我经历相似（similar），我人生中第一次和有相似经历的人交流。注意不能选艰难的，这是一个易误导的选项。
#### 第28题

A. allowed
B. required
C. guided
D. inspired
答案：D
解析：作者勇敢地取下头巾，是被Stephanie的勇敢激励（inspired）的结果。
#### 第29题

A. hide
B. give
C. keep
D. put
答案：A
解析：hide away 藏起来。作者之前一直用头巾掩盖伤疤。
#### 第30题

A. sick
B. awake
C. tired
D. proud
答案：D
解析：be proud of 以……为傲。在与Stephanie交往以后，我对自己的长相不再自卑。

### 2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）

阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的四个选项A、B、C和D中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。
I went to a group activity, "Sensitivity Sunday" which was to make us more __36__ the problems faced by disabled people. We were asked to "__37__ a disability" for several hours one Sunday. Some members __38__ chose the wheelchair. Others wore sound-blocking earplugs (耳塞) or blindfolds (眼罩).
Just sitting in the wheelchair was a __39__ experience. I had never considered before how __40__ it would be to use one. As soon as I sat down my __41__ made the chair begin to roll. Its wheels were not __42__. Then I wondered where to put my __43__. It took me quite a while to get the metal footrest into __44__. I took my first uneasy look at what was to be my only means of __45__ for several hours. For disabled people, "adopting a wheelchair" is not a temporary (临时的) __46__.
I tried to find a __47__ position and thought it might be restful, __48__ kind of nice to be __49__ around for a while. Looking around, I __50__ would have to handle the thing myself! My hands started to ache as I __51__ the heavy wheels. I came to know that controlling the __52__ of the wheelchair was not going to be __53__ task.
My wheelchair experiment was soon __54__. It made a deep impression on me. A few hours of "disability" gave me only a taste of the __55__, both physical and mental, that disabled people must overcome.

#### 第36题

A. curious about
B. interested in
C. aware of
D. careful with
答案：C
解析：活动的目的是为了"意识"到残疾人面临的问题。
#### 第37题

A. cure
B. prevent
C. adopt
D. analyze
答案：C
解析：作者被要求"挑选/采用"一种残疾。
#### 第38题

A. instead
B. strangely
C. as usual
D. like me
答案：D
解析：根据my wheelchair experiment可知，作者选择使用轮椅，即"像我一样"的成员选了轮椅。
#### 第39题

A. learning
B. working
C. satisfying
D. relaxing
答案：A
解析：作者认为坐在轮椅上就是一次"学习"的经历。
#### 第40题

A. convenient
B. awkward
C. boring
D. exciting
答案：B
解析：活动前作者从未意识到使用轮椅有多么的"笨拙"。
#### 第41题

A. height
B. force
C. skill
D. weight
答案：D
解析：一坐下去轮子就动起来，显然是作者的体重让轮子动起来。
#### 第42题

A. locked
B. repaired
C. powered
D. grasped
答案：A
解析：轮子会转动是因为轮子未被锁住。
#### 第43题

A. hands
B. feet
C. keys
D. handles
答案：B
解析：作者不知道把自己的脚放在哪里。
#### 第44题

A. place
B. action
C. play
D. effect
答案：A
解析：put…into place意为"把……归位"。
#### 第45题

A. operation
B. communication
C. transportation
D. production
答案：C
解析：轮椅是一种交通工具。means of transportation。
#### 第46题

A. exploration
B. education
C. experiment
D. entertainment
答案：C
解析：对作者是实验，但对残疾人而言使用轮椅是一辈子的生活，不再是实验。
#### 第47题

A. flexible
B. safe
C. starting
D. comfortable
答案：D
解析：作者努力要找一个舒服的姿势。
#### 第48题

A. yet
B. just
C. still
D. even
答案：D
解析：kind of nice相比restful递进了一步，用even"甚至"。
#### 第49题

A. shown
B. pushed
C. driven
D. guided
答案：B
解析：作者坐着轮椅，自然希望被人推。
#### 第50题

A. realized
B. suggested
C. agreed
D. admitted
答案：A
解析：通过四下张望，作者意识到自己得搞定这件事。
#### 第51题

A. lifted
B. turned
C. pressed
D. seized
答案：B
解析：作者得用手拨动轮椅才能转，手在转动轮子时开始疼。turn 转动。
#### 第52题

A. path
B. position
C. direction
D. way
答案：C
解析：交通工具最重要的是掌控方向。
#### 第53题

A. easy
B. heavy
C. major
D. extra
答案：A
解析：轮子重、手又疼，可推断拨动轮椅不是件容易的工作。
#### 第54题

A. forgotten
B. repeated
C. conducted
D. finished
答案：D
解析：作者要感慨残疾人的不易，自然要到体验结束。
#### 第55题

A. weaknesses
B. challenges
C. anxieties
D. illnesses
答案：B
解析："我"只是品尝了一下残疾人必须要克服的心理和身体上的挑战。

#### 第41题

B. drive along
C. hurry down
D. keep off

#### 第42题

B. people
C. street numbers
D. traffic lights

#### 第43题

B. chooses
C. greets
D. sees

#### 第44题

B. hangs out
C. moves on
D. turns around

解析：给了手套后继续前进，寻找更多需要手套的人。
#### 第45题

A. hands
B. ears
C. faces
D. eyes
答案：A
解析：他送的是手套，要寻找的是手冷的穷人。
#### 第46题

A. searches for
B. stores up
C. gives away
D. puts on
答案：C
解析：再次说他赠送手套。
#### 第47题

A. borrows
B. sells
C. returns
D. buys
答案：D

#### 第48题

A. call
B. send
C. lend
D. show
答案：B

#### 第49题

A. delayed
B. remembered
C. began
D. enjoyed
答案：C
解析：回忆他21年前开始帮助别人。
#### 第50题

A. understand
B. dislike
C. study
D. excuse
答案：A
解析：很多人都熟悉了，因此能够理解他的行为。
#### 第51题

A. sorry for
B. satisfied with
C. proud of
D. surprised by
答案：D
解析：不认识他的人有时会对他感到惊奇。
#### 第52题

A. smart
B. rich
C. special
D. happy
答案：D

#### 第36题

### 2014年上海卷（秋考）

For each blank in the following passage there are four words or phrases marked A, B, C and D. Fill in each blank with the word or phrase that best fits the context.
Research has shown that two-thirds of human conversation is taken up not with discussion of the cultural or political problems of the day, not heated debates about films we've just watched or books we've just finished reading, but plain and simple __51__.
Language is our greatest treasure as a species, and what do we __52__ do with it? We gossip. About others' behaviour and private lives, such as who's doing what with whom, who's in and who's out-and why; how to deal with difficult __53__ situations involving children, lovers, and colleagues.
So why are we keen on gossiping? Are we just natural __54__, of both time and words? Or do we talk a lot about nothing in particular simply to avoid facing up to the really important issues of life? It's not the case according to Professor Robin Dunbar. In fact, in his latest book, Grooming, Gossip and the Evolution of Language, the psychologist says gossip is one of these really__55__issues.
Dunbar __56__ the traditional view that language was developed by the men at the early stage of social development in order to organize their manly hunting activities more effectively, or even to promote the exchange of poetic stories about their origins and the supernatural. Instead he suggests that language evolved among women. We don't spend two-thirds of our time gossiping just because we can talk, argues Dunbar—__57__, he goes on to say, language evolved specifically to allow us to gossip.
Dunbar arrived at his cheery theory by studying the __58__ of the higher primates（灵长类动物）like monkeys. By means of grooming--cleaning the fur by brushing it, monkeys form groups with other individuals on whom they can rely for support in the event of some kind of conflict within the group or__59__ from outside it.
As we human beings evolve from a particular branch of the primate family, Dunbar __60__ that at one time in our history we did much the same. Grouping together made sense because the bigger the group, the greater the __61__ it provided; on the other hand, the bigger the group, the greater the stresses of living close to others. Grooming helped to __62__ the pressure and calm everybody down.
But as the groups got bigger and bigger, the amount of time spent in grooming activities also had to be __63__ to maintain its effectiveness. Clearly, a more __64__ kind of grooming was needed, and thus language evolved as a kind of vocal（有声的）grooming which allowed humans to develop relationship with ever-larger groups by exchanging information over a wider network of individuals than would be possible by one-to-one __65__ contact.

A. claim

As a general rule, all forms of activity lead to boredom when they are performed on a routine basis. As a matter of fact, we can see this __41__ at work in people of all __42__. For example, on Christmas morning, children are excited about __43__ with their new toys. But their __44__ soon wears off and by January those __45__ toys can be found put away in the basement. The world is full of __46__ stamp albums and unfinished models, each standing as a monument to someone's __47__ interest. When parents bring home a pet, their child __48__ bathes it and brushes its fur. Within a short time, however, the __49__ of caring for the animal is handed over to the parents. Adolescents enter high school with great __50__ but are soon looking forward to __51__. The same is true of the young adults going to college. And then, how many __52__, who now complain about the long drives to work, __53__ drove for hours at a time when they first __54__ their driver's licenses? Before people retire, they usually __55__ to do a lot of __56__ things, which they never had __57__ to do while working. But __58__ after retirement, the golfing, the fishing, the reading and all of the other pastimes become as boring as the jobs they __59__. And, like the child in January, they go searching for new __60__.

#### 第41题

A. habit
B. principle
C. way
D. power
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。由首句"as a general rule"，此处指这一普遍原理principle。
#### 第42题

A. parties
B. races
C. countries
D. ages
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。people of all ages各年龄段的人。
#### 第43题

A. working
B. living
C. playing
D. going
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析。play with new toys玩新玩具。
#### 第44题

A. confidence
B. interest
C. anxiety
D. sorrow
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。但他们的兴趣很快消退，interest。
#### 第45题

A. same
B. extra
C. funny
D. expensive
答案：A
解析：考查形容词辨析。到一月那些同样的玩具被收进地下室，same。
#### 第46题

A. well-organized
B. colorfully-printed
C. half-filled
D. newly-collected
答案：C
解析：考查形容词辨析。与unfinished models并列，half-filled stamp albums集了一半的邮册。
#### 第47题

A. broad
B. passing
C. different
D. main
答案：B
解析：考查形容词辨析。每件都是某人短暂兴趣的纪念，passing短暂的。
#### 第48题

A. silently
B. impatiently
C. worriedly
D. gladly
答案：D
解析：考查副词辨析。孩子高兴地给宠物洗澡刷毛，gladly。
#### 第49题

A. promise
B. burden
C. right
D. game
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。照顾动物的重担转交给父母，burden。
#### 第50题

A. courage
B. calmness
C. confusion
D. excitement
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。青少年满怀兴奋进入高中，excitement。
#### 第51题

A. graduation
B. independence
C. responsibility
D. success
答案：A
解析：考查名词辨析。很快又盼着毕业，graduation。
#### 第52题

A. children
B. students
C. adults
D. retirees
答案：C
解析：考查名词辨析。按年龄顺序此处指成年人，adults。
#### 第53题

A. carefully
B. eagerly
C. nervously
D. bravely
答案：B
解析：考查副词辨析。刚拿到驾照时热切地一开就是几小时，eagerly。
#### 第54题

A. required
B. obtained
C. noticed
D. discovered
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。obtain a driver's license获得驾照。
#### 第55题

A. need
B. learn
C. plan
D. start
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析。退休前他们通常计划做很多事情，plan。
#### 第56题

A. great
B. strange
C. difficult
D. correct
答案：A
解析：考查形容词辨析。计划做很多很棒的事情，great。
#### 第57题

A. time
B. money
C. skills
D. knowledge
答案：A
解析：考查名词辨析。工作时没有时间做这些事，time。
#### 第58题

A. only
B. well
C. even
D. soon
答案：D
解析：考查副词辨析。但退休后不久，这些消遣也变得无聊，soon。
#### 第59题

A. lost
B. chose
C. quit
D. left
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。像他们离开的工作一样无聊，left。
#### 第60题

A. pets
B. toys
C. friends
D. colleagues
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。像一月的孩子一样，他们去寻找新玩具，与开头呼应。
### 2014年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）

阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的四个选项（A、B、C和D）中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。
Joe Simpson and Simon Yates were the first people to climb the West Face of the Siula Grande in the Andes mountains. They reached the top __41__, but on their way back conditions were very __42__. Joe fell and broke his leg. They both knew that if Simon __43__ alone, he would probably get back __44__. But Simon decided to risk his __45__ and try to lower Joe down the mountain on a rope.
As they __46__ down, the weather got worse. Then another __47__ occurred. They couldn't see or hear each other and, __48__, Simon lowered his friend over the edge of a precipice. It was __49__ for Joe to climb back or for Simon to pull him up. Joe's __50__ was pulling Simon slowly towards the precipice. __51__, after more than an hour in the dark and the icy cold, Simon had to __52__. In tears, he cut the rope. Joe __53__ into a large crevasse in the ice below. He had no food or water and he was in terrible pain. He couldn't walk, but he __54__ to get out of the crevasse and started to __55__ towards their camp, nearly ten kilometers __56__.
Simon had __57__ the camp at the foot of the mountain. He thought that Joe must be __58__, but he didn't want to leave __59__. Three days later, in the middle of the night, he heard Joe's voice. He couldn't __60__ it. Joe was there, a few meters from their tent, still alive.

#### 第41题

A. hurriedly
B. carefully
C. successfully
D. early
答案：C
解析：考查副词辨析。他们成功登顶，successfully。
#### 第42题

A. difficult
B. similar
C. special
D. normal
答案：A
解析：考查形容词辨析。但下山时情况很艰难，difficult。
#### 第43题

A. climbed
B. worked
C. rested
D. continued
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。如果Simon独自继续下山，他可能安全返回，continue。
#### 第44题

A. unwillingly
B. safely
C. slowly
D. regretfully
答案：B
解析：考查副词辨析。丢下受伤同伴，他能安全返回，safely。
#### 第45题

A. fortune
B. time
C. health
D. life
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。Simon决定冒生命危险用绳子带他下山，life。
#### 第46题

A. lay
B. settled
C. went
D. looked
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析。他们下山时天气变坏，go down下山。
#### 第47题

A. damage
B. storm
C. change
D. trouble
答案：D
解析：考查名词辨析。又一个麻烦发生了，trouble。
#### 第48题

A. by mistake
B. by chance
C. by choice
D. by luck
答案：A
解析：考查短语辨析。他们看不清听不见，Simon误把朋友放到了峭壁边，by mistake。
#### 第49题

A. unnecessary
B. practical
C. important
D. impossible
答案：D
解析：考查形容词辨析。Joe爬不上来，Simon也拉不上他，impossible。
#### 第50题

A. height
B. weight
C. strength
D. equipment
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。Joe的重量把Simon慢慢往峭壁拉，weight。
#### 第51题

A. Finally
B. Patiently
C. Surely
D. Quickly
答案：A
解析：考查副词辨析。在黑暗严寒中熬了一个多小时后，Simon最终不得不做决定，finally。
#### 第52题

A. stand back
B. take a rest
C. make a decision
D. hold on
答案：C
解析：考查动词短语。他不得不做出（割断绳子的）决定，make a decision。
#### 第53题

A. jumped
B. fell
C. escaped
D. backed
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。Joe跌进下面冰上的大裂缝，fall。
#### 第54题

A. managed
B. planned
C. waited
D. hoped
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。他设法爬出裂缝，manage to do设法做成。
#### 第55题

A. run
B. skate
C. move
D. march
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析。他受伤了，只能慢慢向营地移动，move。
#### 第56题

A. around
B. away
C. above
D. along
答案：B
解析：考查副词辨析。营地离这儿近十公里远，away表距离。
#### 第57题

A. headed for
B. travelled
C. left for
D. returned to
答案：D
解析：考查动词短语。Simon已回到山脚的营地，return to。
#### 第58题

A. dead
B. hurt
C. weak
D. late
答案：A
解析：考查形容词辨析。他以为Joe一定死了，dead。
#### 第59题

A. secretly
B. tiredly
C. immediately
D. anxiously
答案：C
解析：考查副词辨析。但他没有立即离开，immediately。
#### 第60题

A. find
B. believe
C. make
D. accept
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。他没想到Joe会活着回来，不敢相信，believe。
## 2015年

### 2015年北京卷

阅读下面短文，掌握其大意，从每题所给的A、B、C、D四个选项中，选出最佳选项。
A Welcome Gift
Dario and his mother loved their new apartment. The living room was large enough for their piano. That night, the two of them (36) ____ side by side at the piano. They played jazz music to celebrate their new home. The loud (37) ____ filled the room and made them feel very happy.
The next morning, (38) ____, their happiness disappeared. Someone had left a (39) ____ under their door during the night. One of their neighbor had written to complain(抱怨) about the sound of the piano. Dario's mother asked the building superintendent(管理员) if he knew anything about it. But he said that they were all (40) ____ people and he couldn't imagine any of them had done that. Later that morning, Dario suggested that they write a letter to their (41) ____ and apologize for their playing.
"Maybe we could go and (42) ____ everyone in person." his mother said.
"What if we invited them to come here for a (43) ____ instead?" Dario asked.
They both loved the (44) ____. Over the next few days, they sent out invitations and prepared desserts (45) ____ their guests. They decorated the apartment with streamers(彩带) and party lights.
Finally, the day of the party (46) ____. Some guests brought presents. Others brought flowers. Some even brought desserts to (47) ____. One woman, Mrs. Gilbert, (48) ____ Dario's mother with a book of piano music by Chopin.
"I heard you playing the other night," she said. "The sounds woke me out of bed. I (49) ____ that you might play like this every night. So I wrote a short note. I hope you don't think I disliked the playing."
Dario's mother smiled at Mrs. Gilbert. "I think maybe we (50) ____ you an apology." she said. "I didn't (51) ____ how late it was when we were playing. Maybe we should play some quieter music at night."
"You play, you play!" Mrs. Gilbert said. "I like what you play! Just not so loud at night." She pointed to the book she had given them. "These songs are not such (52) ____ music."
"These songs are beautiful music." Dario's mother said. "We will be (53) ____ to play them in the evening."
"And we won't play so loud or late!" Dario said. He was already looking forward to (54) ____ the new music. More than that, however, he was happy to see the big smile on his mother's face. It gave him a feeling of (55) ____ and made him feel that they were home at last.

#### 第36题

A. sat
B. stood
C. lay
D. walked
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。根据"side by side at the piano"可知他们是坐在钢琴旁边弹奏。
#### 第37题

A. voice
B. ring
C. music
D. cry
答案：C
解析：考查名词辨析。由上文"They played jazz music"可知是吵闹的音乐充满房间。
#### 第38题

A. therefore
B. however
C. otherwise
D. instead
答案：B
解析：考查副词辨析。上文"made them feel very happy"与下文"their happiness disappeared"是转折关系。
#### 第39题

A. note
B. poster
C. bill
D. report
答案：A
解析：考查名词辨析。由下文"written to complain about the sound of the piano"可知是一张便条。
#### 第40题

A. proud
B. rich
C. lucky
D. nice
答案：D
解析：考查形容词辨析。管理员说大家通常都是通情达理的，他也想不出谁会那么做。
#### 第41题

A. neighbors
B. friends
C. relatives
D. audience
答案：A
解析：考查名词辨析。邻居留便条抱怨，他们应给邻居们写信致歉。
#### 第42题

A. blame
B. instruct
C. question
D. visit
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。go and visit everyone in person意为亲自拜访每一个人。
#### 第43题

A. party
B. concert
C. show
D. play
答案：A
解析：考查名词辨析。由下文"party lights""the day of the party"原词复现可知是举办聚会。
#### 第44题

A. experience
B. idea
C. performance
D. action
答案：B
解析：考查名词辨析。母子打算办派对，这是一个主意。
#### 第45题

A. to
B. with
C. for
D. from
答案：C
解析：考查介词辨析。prepare sth for sb为某人准备某物。
#### 第46题

A. continued
B. arrived
C. passed
D. finished
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。由下文"Some guests brought presents"可知聚会这一天终于到来了。
#### 第47题

A. order
B. sell
C. share
D. advertise
答案：C
解析：考查动词辨析。参加聚会，一些邻居带甜点来分享。
#### 第48题

A. treated
B. presented
C. helped
D. served
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。present sb with sth把某物赠予某人，Mrs. Gilbert送给Dario的妈妈一本音乐书。
#### 第49题

A. promised
B. admitted
C. agreed
D. worried
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。由"you might play like this every night"可知她当时很担心每天晚上都会这么吵。
#### 第50题

A. give
B. send
C. offer
D. owe
答案：D
解析：考查动词辨析。owe sb an apology意为应该向某人道歉。
#### 第51题

A. realize
B. remember
C. understand
D. accept
答案：A
解析：考查动词辨析。Dario的妈妈道歉说没意识到当时那么晚了还在弹钢琴。
#### 第52题

A. sweet
B. strange
C. funny
D. loud
答案：D
解析：考查形容词辨析。由"Just not so loud at night"可知她送的是不那么吵的音乐书。
#### 第53题

A. brave
B. sorry
C. happy
D. afraid
答案：C
解析：考查形容词辨析。由"These songs are beautiful music"可知他们很乐意晚上弹奏。
#### 第54题

A. changing
B. practicing
C. recording
D. writing
答案：B
解析：考查动词辨析。因为是新音乐，Dario期盼练习。
#### 第55题

A. equality
B. freedom
C. warmth
D. sympathy
答案：C
解析：考查名词辨析。由"made him feel that they were home at last"可知有温暖的感觉。
### 2015年上海卷（秋考）

For each blank in the following passage there are four words or phrases marked A, B, C and D. Fill in each blank with the word or phrase that best fits the context.
If you studied pictures that ancient people left on rock walls and you tried to determine their meaning, you would not detect interest in romance among the artists. (51) _____, you would see plenty of animals with people running after them. Life for ancient people's earned to center on hunting and gathering wild foods for meals.
In modern times, when food is available in grocery stores, finding love is more (52) _____ in people's lives. The (53) _____ is all around us. It is easy to prepare a list of modern stories having to do with love. An endless number of books and movies qualify as love stories in popular culture.
Researchers are studying whether love, a highly valued emotional state, can be (54) _____. They ask, what is love? Toothpaste companies want us to think attraction is all about clean teeth, but clean teeth go only so far. Scientists wonder how much the brain gets involved. You have probably heard that opposites attract but that (55) _____ attract, too. One thing is certain: The truth about love is not yet set in stone.
First Impression
To help determine the (56) _____ of attraction, researchers paired 164 college classmates and had them talk for 3, 6 or 10 minutes so they could get a sense of each other's individuality. Then students were asked to (57) _____ what kind of relationship they were likely to build with their partners. After nine weeks, they reported what happened.
As it turned out, their (58) _____ judgments often held true. Students seemed to (59) _____ at an early stage who would best fit into their lives.
The (60) _____ Knows
Scientists have also turned to nonhumans to increase understanding of attraction. Many animals give off pheromones — natural chemicals that can be detected by, and then can produce a response in, other animals of the same species. Pheromones can signal that an animal is either ready to fight or is feeling (61) _____ to partnerships. In contrast, humans do not seem to be as (62) _____ as other animals at detecting such chemicals. Smell, however, does seem to play a part in human attraction. Although we may not be aware of chemicals like pheromones consciously, we give and receive loads of information through smell in every interaction with other people.
Face Value
Being fond of someone seems to have a number of factors, including seeing something we find attractive. Researchers had people judge faces for (63) _____. The participants had 0.013 seconds to view each face, yet somehow they generally considered the images the same as people who had more time to study the same faces. The way we (64) _____ attractiveness seem to be somewhat automatic.
When shown an attractive face and then words with good or bad associations, people responded to (65) _____ words faster after viewing an attractive face. Seeing something attractive seems to cause happy thinking.

#### 第51题

A. Instead
B. Therefore
C. Moreover
D. Otherwise
答案：A
解析：考查副词。前句you would not detect…后句you would see…结构相同，前有not后无，意思相反，故用Instead（相反）。

#### 第52题

A. romantic
B. stressful
C. central
D. artificial
答案：C
解析：考查形容词。
#### 第53题

A. priority
B. proof
C. possibility
D. principle
答案：B
解析：考查名词。后两句列举大量爱情故事、电影，是"证明"的例子，故用proof。
#### 第54题

A. tested
B. impressed
C. changed
D. created
答案：D
解析：考查动词。由末句"The truth about love is not yet set in stone"可知科学家研究爱是否能被"创造"。
#### 第55题

A. appearances
B. virtues
C. similarities
D. passions
答案：C
解析：考查名词。与opposites相对，此处指"相似者相吸"。
#### 第56题

A. illustrations
B. implications
C. ingredients
D. intentions
答案：C
解析：考查名词。实验目的是确定吸引力的"成分"。
#### 第57题

A. predict
B. investigate
C. diagnose
D. recall
答案：A
解析：考查动词。九周后才报道发生的事，说明是"预测"。
#### 第58题

A. critical
B. initial
C. random
D. mature
答案：B
解析：考查形容词。预测的判断是"最初的"。
#### 第59题

A. memorize
B. distinguish
C. negotiate
D. question
答案：B
解析：考查动词。学生似乎能在早期"区别"出谁最适合自己。
#### 第60题

A. Nose
B. Eye
C. Heart
D. Hand
答案：A
解析：考查名词。本段讲嗅觉pheromones，小标题为The Nose Knows。
#### 第61题

A. open
B. alert
C. resistant
D. superior
答案：A
解析：考查形容词。either…or…反义表达，与fight相对的是open to partnerships。
#### 第62题

A. disappointed
B. amazed
C. confused
D. gifted
答案：D
解析：考查形容词。人类在察觉这类化学物质方面不如动物"有天赋"。
#### 第63题

A. emotion
B. attractiveness
C. individuality
D. signals
答案：B
解析：考查名词。与上文attractive同根重现。
#### 第64题

A. enhance
B. possess
C. maintain
D. assess
答案：D
解析：考查动词。我们"评估"吸引力的方式似乎是自动的。
#### 第65题

A. familiar
B. plain
C. positive
D. irritating
答案：C
解析：考查形容词。看到有吸引力的事物会导致快乐思考，故对"积极的"词语反应更快。
阅读下面短文，掌握其大意，然后从16-35各题所给的A、B、C、D四个选项中选出最佳选项。
My fiance(未婚夫)and I were excited about shopping for our first home. But our funds were (16) ______, and none of the houses in our price range seemed satisfactory.
One agent (17) ______ a house in particular. Although her description sounded wonderful, the price was (18) ______ our range, so we declined. But she kept urging us to have a look (19) ______.
We finally did and it was (20) ______ at first sight. It was Our Home, small and charming, overlooking a quiet lake. Walking through the rooms and talking with the owners, a nice elderly couple, we felt the warmth and (21) ______ of the marriage within that home. As perfect as it was, the price remained too high for us. But every day, we would sit by the lake, looking at the house and dreaming of (22) ______ it would be like to live there.
Days later, we made a(n)(23) ______ — far below the asking price. Surprisingly, they didn't (24) ______ us. They renewed their offer (25) ______. It was also much more than we could afford, but far (26) ______ than the original asking price.
The next day, we got a (27) ______ message that another buyer had offered a much higher price. Even so, we decided to talk with the (28) ______ directly. We made our final offer, which (29) ______ was thousands of dollars less than the other buyer's bid. We knew it, (30) ______ we had to try.
"Sold!" said the owner. Then he (31) ______: He'd seen us sitting by the lake all those times; he knew how much we loved the place and that we'd (32) ______ the years of work they had put into their home; he realized he would take a (33) ______ by selling it to us, but it was worthwhile; we were the people they wanted to live there. He told us to consider the (34) ______ in the price "an early wedding present."
That's how we found our home and how I learned that when people are (35) ______ they are not strangers, only friends we haven't yet met.
#### 第16题

A. needed
B. limited
C. enough
D. large
答案：B
解析：形容词辨析。由后文none of the houses in our price range可知资金有限。
#### 第17题

A. recommended
B. decorated
C. sold
D. rented
答案：A
解析：动词辨析。代理人特别向我们推荐一栋房子。
#### 第18题

A. below
B. within
C. beyond
D. between
答案：C
解析：介词辨析。价格超过了我们的范围，所以拒绝。beyond超出。
#### 第19题

A. at least
B. at most
C. at times
D. at hand
答案：A
解析：介词短语。她催促我们至少去看一看。
#### 第20题

A. relief
B. concern
C. love
D. curiosity
答案：C
解析：名词辨析。我们对这房子一见钟情。love at first sight。
#### 第21题

A. pride
B. happiness
C. challenge
D. desire
答案：B
解析：名词辨析。我们感到家中婚姻的温馨与快乐。
#### 第22题

A. which
B. why
C. that
D. what
答案：D
解析：宾语从句连接词。be like缺宾语，用what。
#### 第23题

A. effort
B. offer
C. promise
D. profit
答案：B
解析：名词辨析。make an offer出价。
#### 第24题

A. come across
B. look after
C. depend on
D. laugh at
答案：D
解析：动词短语。令人惊讶的是，他们没有嘲笑我们（出价低）。
#### 第25题

A. instead
B. indeed
C. aside
D. apart
答案：A
解析：副词辨析。相反，他们更新了出价。
#### 第26题

A. worse
B. better
C. less
D. higher
答案：C
解析：形容词辨析。比原来要价少得多。
#### 第27题

A. relaxing
B. disappointing
C. pleasant
D. regular
答案：B
解析：形容词辨析。另一买家出更高价，这是令人失望的消息。
#### 第28题

A. agents
B. buyers
C. managers
D. owners
答案：D
解析：名词辨析。我们决定直接跟房东谈。
#### 第29题

A. already
B. still
C. generally
D. ever
答案：B
解析：副词辨析。我们最终的出价仍然比另一买家低几千。
#### 第30题

A. so
B. or
C. for
D. but
答案：D
解析：连词辨析。我们知道出价低，但不得不试一下。转折关系。
#### 第31题

A. apologized
B. complained
C. criticized
D. explained
答案：D
解析：动词辨析。房东同意卖，然后解释了原因。
#### 第32题

A. check
B. analyze
C. appreciate
D. ignore
答案：C
解析：动词辨析。我们会珍惜他们为房子付出多年的努力。
#### 第33题

A. loss
B. risk
C. chance
D. lead
答案：A
解析：名词辨析。把房子低价卖给我们他会有损失。
#### 第34题

A. increase
B. difference
C. interest
D. average
答案：B
解析：名词辨析。他让我们把价格差额当作提前的结婚礼物。
#### 第35题

A. kind
B. polite
C. smart
D. energetic
答案：A
解析：形容词辨析。当人们善良时，他们不是陌生人，只是还没遇见的朋友。

As executives(高级管理人员)with banks, consulting firms, established law firms, and major corporations, many are now (21) ______ on their way to impressive careers. As they left student life behind, many had a (23) ______ drink at their cheap but friendly local bar, shook hands with longtime roommates, and (24) ______ out of small apartments into high buildings. They made reservations at restaurants where the cost of a bottle of wine (25) ______ a college year's monthly rent. They replaced their beloved old car with expensive new sports cars.
The thing is, a number of them have (26) ______ that despite their success, they aren't happy. Some (27) ______ of unfriendly coworkers and feel sad for eight-hour workweeks devoted to tasks they (28) ______. Some do not respect the companies they work for and talk of feeling tired and (29) ______. However, instead of devoting themselves to their work, they find themselves working to support the (30) ______ to which they have so quickly become (31) ______.

### 2015年福建卷

阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的四个选项（A、B、C、D）中，选出最佳选项。

Several years ago I (37) ______ a letter from seventeen-year-old Kerry, who described herself as a world-class fault-finder, almost always (38) ______ by things. People were always doing things that annoyed her, and (39) ______ was ever good enough. She was highly self-critical and also found fault with her friends. She became a really (40) ______ person.
Unfortunately, it took a horrible accident to change her (41) ______. Her best friend was seriously hurt in a car crash. What made it almost (42) ______ to deal with was that the day before the (43) ______, Kerry had visited her friend and had spent the whole time criticizing her (44) ______ of boyfriends, the way she was living, the way she related to her mother, and various other things she felt she needed to (45) ______. It wasn't until her friend was badly hurt that Kerry became (46) ______ her habit of finding fault. Very quickly, she learned to appreciate life rather than to (47) ______ everything so harshly(刻薄). She was able to transfer her new wisdom to other parts of her (48) ______ as well.
Perhaps most of us aren't as extreme at fault-finding, (49) ______ when we're honest, we can be sharply (50) ______ of the world. I'm not suggesting you (51) ______ problems, or that you pretend things are (52) ______ than they are, but simply that you learn to allow things to be as they are — (53) ______ most of the time, and especially when it's not a really big (54) ______.

It was a rainy morning and the children, mainly boys with various learning difficulties, refused to settle for the start of the lesson. As an inexperienced teacher, I tried every means to get them to be (36) ______, but in vain. My panic was rising and I could feel my heart beating wildly. Hoping that no one would notice that I was (38) ______ inside, I threw my voice as far as it would reach: "Put your heads on the desks and close your (39) ______! We are going on a journey."
(40) ______, the children fell silent. "Now what should I do?" I thought to myself. Reaching over to my collection of CDs, I blindly (41) ______, put it in the machine and played it.
Obediently(顺从地), my class lay their heads on their desk, closed their eyes and (42) ______. When the music started, the room was filled with the most beautiful tones and musical colors I could have ever imagined. All the children were (43) ______. When the music finished, I asked them all to raise their (44) ______ slowly so that we could share our musical journey.
At this point, when all the children were willing to share their experiences, I began to learn how to (45) ______. The music allowed me to learn that teaching is about sharing and respect, tears and smiles, the knowing and the (46) ______ and most of all, an understanding of each other. This was the power that (47) ______ in the classroom could have.

阅读下面短文，掌握其大意，然后从1-15各题所给的A、B、C和D项中，选出最佳选项。（原卷未附本大题答案字母）

When does old age begin then? Sixty-five may be out-of-date as the (6) ______ line between middle age and old age. After all, many older people don't begin to experience physical and mental (7) ______ until after age 75.
People are living longer because more people (8) ______ childhood. Before modern medicine changed the laws of nature, many children died of common childhood (9) ______. Now that the chances of dying (10) ______ are much lower, the chances of living long are much higher due to better diets and health care.
On the whole, our population is getting older. The (11) ______ in our population will have lasting effects on our social development and our way of life. Some people fear such changes will be for the worse, while some see (12) ______, not disaster. Many men and women in their "golden years" are healthy, still active, and young in (13) ______ if not in age.
As the society grows old, we need the (14) ______ of our older citizens. With long lives ahead of them, they need to (15) ______ active and devoted.

### 2015年四川卷

阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的四个选项（A、B、C、D）中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。
My previous home had a stand of woods behind it and many animals in the backyard. That first year, I (11) ______ feeding peanuts to the blue jays, then the squirrels. The squirrels had no (12) ______ coming up right to me for them. As the months went by, the rabbits saw that I was no (13) ______ and didn't escape. When I threw carrot slices(薄片), they even came for a nibble(啃). Slowly they came to (14) ______ me, and by the end of the year they were eating out of my hand.
That second year, the rabbits (15) ______ me, and one would even sit up for slices! While I was feeding them, I (16) ______ that a groundhog who used to run away was now taking an (17) ______ interest in this food situation. I carefully extended a long (18) ______, with a keen eye on those teeth, and (19) ______, there were times I would have the groundhog sitting next to a rabbit, both munching(津津有味地咀嚼)on carrots. A few months later, while (20) ______, she would even turn her back to me. (21) ______ when she was facing away, I reached out and (22) ______ scratched(搔)her back with my finger. She didn't move.
By year three, the rabbits and the groundhog were back. The groundhog (23) ______ didn't have a problem with me scratching her back, and I got an idea. I'd always (24) ______, while slicing up carrots, that the end looked like a cap. (25) ______ one day, just to see what she would do, I gently (26) ______ one on top of the groundhog's head. Again, not a (27) ______. The next time, I had my camera ready to record what you see here, one of several dozen such pictures. (28) ______ she had a slice to eat, she never (29) ______ the one on her head. It was a fair (30) ______ — I got a pleasure, and she had yet another tasty treat.

#### 第11题

B. started
